## 阻擋

### R357-01／R354-01 補充：來源登記已補，但裁切求值的責任層級與公理仍有矛盾

[本次新增登記](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/cd4bc8803016943e6ec7bf98f0c90bc0312cfa06/geometry-core/domain-decoupling/product-architecture.json#L2039-L2055)把 `cutting-plan.js`、`cutting-settings.js` 全列為 H。可是：

- [公理分層契約](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676/公理層.md#L63-L86)明列 D46–D50、I58／I59／I61、R47–R49 由 W 負責；[I60 的共用守恆機制](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676/公理層.md#L1739-L1754)另屬 K。
- [cutting-plan.js:57–95](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/cd4bc8803016943e6ec7bf98f0c90bc0312cfa06/geometry-web/cutting-plan.js#L57-L95)直接選餘料、排序件、先適配遞減排料、算末刀／端修、分類剩料及驗守恆；沒有呼叫固定 W 求值者。這不只是從 H 讀資料、編排呼叫或輸出 CSV。
- [cutting-settings.js:9–40](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/cd4bc8803016943e6ec7bf98f0c90bc0312cfa06/geometry-web/cutting-settings.js#L9-L40)除了 H 的附件與高水位，亦直接決定材料／無序截面配對、鋸縫／留用下限、可用料長及原材在用保護。
- 不能只把 `cutting-plan.js` 改標 W：[第 3 行](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/cd4bc8803016943e6ec7bf98f0c90bc0312cfa06/geometry-web/cutting-plan.js#L1-L3)仍直接依賴既有 H `timber-schedule.js`，會形成 W→H；[現行裁判的依賴規則](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/cd4bc8803016943e6ec7bf98f0c90bc0312cfa06/tests/architecture_contract.py#L97-L98)不允許這條邊。

這不是說每個實體檔必須只含一種條文，也不是 #357 新引入數值回歸。演算法是 #354 回收進來；**本 PR 新增的責任分類，尚不能把該回收程式認定為已符合現行 K/W/H 契約。** [架構文件本身](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/cd4bc8803016943e6ec7bf98f0c90bc0312cfa06/geometry-core/domain-decoupling/PRODUCT-ARCHITECTURE.md#L12-L15)亦明示依賴掃描不能代替演算法責任審查，`0 diagnostics` 不解除這項矛盾。

**建議的最小責任界線：**限於此次回收的裁切模組，H 保留一次原生讀取、既有料單導出、專案附件、session 與 CSV；以明確需求資料呼叫固定 W 的配料／FFD／耗損分類與設定政策；I60 共用守恆機制按 K 契約承接。公開能力、格式、拒絕規則、數值及 CSV bytes 保持原契約。這是實作建議，不擅改公理，也不要求將全庫其餘 H 程式重構後才處理本片。

**本輪未確認：**完整七 jobs 終態、修改後 P89 真瀏覽器／390px、獨立 main fixture 重產、完整原生及 sanitizer、實體手機／Windows；上述尚未由本輪局部結果證成。既有 R354-02～08 在已核源碼與已回報專項範圍可收斂，不再列為原樣未修。

+- H `cutting-plan.js` 保留原生快照及既有 `timber-schedule.js` 的需求轉接、公開結果包裝與 CSV；`cutting-settings.js` 保留附件 schema、key 高水位、歷史保存及 canonical fingerprint，領域政策固定委派 W。H 不再持有 FFD、材料配對或耗損分類實作。
+- H `geometry-app/application.js` 無條件組裝 frozen `woodworking/1` 裁切 provider；缺少固定 W 就拒絕啟動，沒有選擇器、停用入口或任意插件註冊。公開呼叫、附件格式、requires 與 CSV 契約不因此分層新增版本。

--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-core/interval-allocation.js
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+/* I60: neutral exact one-dimensional allocation conservation. All intervals,
+   consumption and usable bounds are explicit inputs; no domain policy/defaults.
+   Identity records are own data properties on local plain/null-prototype objects;
+   data from another realm must cross as JSON, not as an opaque live object. */
+(function (root, factory) {
+  'use strict';
+  const api = factory();
+  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
+  else Object.defineProperty(root, 'GeometryIntervalAllocation', {value: api});
+})(this, function () {
+  'use strict';
+  function fail(code, reason) { throw Object.assign(Error(code + ': ' + reason), {code, reason}); }
+  const integer = value => Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0;
+  function canonical(value, ancestors = new Set()) {
+    if (value === null || typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'boolean') return JSON.stringify(value);
+    if (typeof value === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(value)) return JSON.stringify(value);
+    if (value && typeof value === 'object' && ancestors.has(value)) fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'invalid-key');
+    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
+      if (Object.keys(value).length !== value.length || Object.getOwnPropertyNames(value).length !== value.length + 1 ||
+          Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(value).length) fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'invalid-key');
+      const nested = new Set(ancestors); nested.add(value);
+      return '[' + Array.from({length: value.length}, (_, index) => {
+        const property = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, String(index));
+        if (!property?.enumerable || !Object.hasOwn(property, 'value')) fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'invalid-key');
+        return canonical(property.value, nested);
+      }).join(',') + ']';
+    }
+    if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(value).length) {
+      const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
+      if (prototype !== null && prototype !== Object.prototype ||
+          Object.getOwnPropertyNames(value).length !== Object.keys(value).length) fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'invalid-key');
+      const nested = new Set(ancestors); nested.add(value);
+      return '{' + Object.keys(value).sort().map(key => {
+        const property = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, key);
+        if (!property.enumerable || !Object.hasOwn(property, 'value')) fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'invalid-key');
+        return JSON.stringify(key) + ':' + canonical(property.value, nested);
+      }).join(',') + '}';
+    }
+    fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'invalid-key');
+  }
+  function identity(key) {
+    const type = key && Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(key, 'type');
+    const id = key && Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(key, 'id');
+    if (!key || typeof key !== 'object' || Array.isArray(key) ||
+        !type?.enumerable || !id?.enumerable || !Object.hasOwn(type, 'value') || !Object.hasOwn(id, 'value') ||
+        typeof type.value !== 'string' || !type.value ||
+        !(integer(id.value) || typeof id.value === 'string' && id.value.length)) fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'invalid-key');
+    return canonical(key);
+  }
+  function createIdentityTracker() {
+    const identities = new Set();
+    function add(key) {
+      const id = identity(key);
+      if (identities.has(id)) fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'duplicate');
+      identities.add(id);
+    }
+    function has(key) { return identities.has(identity(key)); }
+    return Object.freeze({add: Object.freeze(add), has: Object.freeze(has), get size() { return identities.size; }});
+  }
+  function verifyAllocation(allocation, usable) {
+    if (!allocation || !integer(allocation.length) || allocation.length === 0 ||
+        !integer(allocation.start) || !integer(allocation.end) || allocation.end <= allocation.start ||
+        !Array.isArray(usable) || usable.length !== 2 || !usable.every(integer) || usable[0] > usable[1] ||
+        BigInt(allocation.end) - BigInt(allocation.start) !== BigInt(allocation.length) ||
+        allocation.start < usable[0] || allocation.end > usable[1]) fail('CONSERVATION', 'invalid-demand');
+    return true;
+  }
+  function verify(value) {
+    if (!value || !Array.isArray(value.expected) || !Array.isArray(value.containers)) fail('CONSERVATION', 'invalid-allocation');
+    const expected = createIdentityTracker(), actual = createIdentityTracker();
+    for (const key of value.expected) expected.add(key);
+    for (const container of value.containers) {
+      if (!container || !integer(container.length) || container.length === 0 ||
+          !Array.isArray(container.usable) || container.usable.length !== 2 ||
+          !container.usable.every(integer) || container.usable[0] > container.usable[1] ||
+          container.usable[1] > container.length || !Array.isArray(container.allocations) ||
+          !Array.isArray(container.consumptions) || !container.remainder) fail('CONSERVATION', 'invalid-container');
+      const intervals = [];
+      let total = 0n;
+      function interval(segment, positive) {
+        if (!segment || !integer(segment.start) || !integer(segment.end) ||
+            segment.end < segment.start || positive && segment.end === segment.start ||
+            segment.end > container.length) fail('CONSERVATION', 'invalid-interval');
+        const length = BigInt(segment.end) - BigInt(segment.start);
+        total += length;
+        if (length > 0n) intervals.push(segment);
+        return length;
+      }
+      for (const allocation of container.allocations) {
+        actual.add(allocation?.key);
+        verifyAllocation(allocation, container.usable);
+        interval(allocation, true);
+      }
+      for (const consumption of container.consumptions) interval(consumption, false);
+      interval(container.remainder, false);
+      if (container.remainder.end !== container.length) fail('CONSERVATION', 'invalid-interval');
+      intervals.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - b.end);
+      for (let index = 1; index < intervals.length; index++) {
+        if (intervals.at(index).start < intervals.at(index - 1).end) fail('CONSERVATION', 'overlap');
+      }
+      if (total !== BigInt(container.length)) fail('CONSERVATION', 'unequal-total');
+    }
+    if (actual.size !== expected.size || value.expected.some(key => !actual.has(key))) fail('DEMAND_IDENTITY', 'missing');
+    return true;
+  }
+  return Object.freeze({verify: Object.freeze(verify), createIdentityTracker: Object.freeze(createIdentityTracker), verifyAllocation: Object.freeze(verifyAllocation)});
+});

+/* Fixed woodworking/1 cutting policy. Inputs are explicit native-derived rows
+   and declarations; this provider never imports host stores, schedules or UI. */
+(function(root,factory){
+ 'use strict';
+ const api=factory(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports?require('../../geometry-core/interval-allocation.js'):root.GeometryIntervalAllocation);
+ if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;
+ else Object.defineProperty(root,'GeometryWoodworkingCutting',{value:api});
+})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this,Core=>{
+ 'use strict';
+ if(!Core||typeof Core.verify!=='function'||!Object.isFrozen(Core))throw Error('固定裁切模板缺少共用區間驗證器。');
+ const uint=n=>Number.isSafeInteger(n)&&n>=0&&n<=4294967295,key=n=>uint(n)&&n>0;
+ const integer=(n,min=0)=>Number.isSafeInteger(n)&&n>=min&&n<=100000000;
+ function fail(code,detail){throw Object.assign(Error(`${code}：${detail}`),{code});}
+ function keys(v,required){return v!==null&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v)&&Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(v).length===0&&Object.keys(v).length===required.length&&required.every(k=>Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(v,k)?.enumerable&&Object.hasOwn(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(v,k),'value'));}
+ const materialKey=m=>m===null?'unset':JSON.stringify([m.id,m.version,m.fingerprint]);
+ const sectionKey=s=>[...s].sort((a,b)=>a-b).join('x');
+ const stockIdentity=s=>sectionKey(s.section)+':'+materialKey(s.material);
+ const sameMaterial=(a,b)=>materialKey(a)===materialKey(b);
+ const validSettingsFields=v=>(v.kerf===null||integer(v.kerf,1))&&(v.allowance===null||integer(v.allowance))&&(v.minRemnant===null||integer(v.minRemnant,1));
+ function validateDeclarations(stockTypes,declaredRemnants){
+  if(!Array.isArray(stockTypes)||!Array.isArray(declaredRemnants)||stockTypes.length>32||declaredRemnants.length>128)fail('CAPACITY','原材至多 32 種、宣告餘料至多 128 段。');
+  const stocks=new Map(),identities=new Set(),remnants=new Set();
+  for(const s of stockTypes){
+   if(!keys(s,['key','name','section','material','length','endTrim'])||!key(s.key)||typeof s.name!=='string'||!s.name.length||new TextEncoder().encode(s.name).length>96||
+     !Array.isArray(s.section)||s.section.length!==2||!s.section.every(n=>integer(n,1))||!integer(s.length,1)||!integer(s.endTrim)||s.endTrim>=s.length/2)fail('STOCK_INVALID','原材欄位、名稱、截面或可用長度不合法。');
+   if(s.material!==null&&(!keys(s.material,['id','version','fingerprint'])||typeof s.material.id!=='string'||!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(s.material.id)||!key(s.material.version)||!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(s.material.fingerprint)))fail('MATERIAL_INVALID','材料鍵須為完整的固定版本與內容指紋。');
+   if(stocks.has(s.key)||identities.has(stockIdentity(s)))fail('STOCK_DUPLICATE','原材 key 或截面與材料鍵重複。');
+   stocks.set(s.key,s);identities.add(stockIdentity(s));
+  }
+  for(const r of declaredRemnants){
+   if(!keys(r,['key','stockType','length','headTrim','tailTrim'])||!key(r.key)||!key(r.stockType)||!stocks.has(r.stockType)||!integer(r.length,1)||!integer(r.headTrim)||!integer(r.tailTrim)||r.length-r.headTrim-r.tailTrim<1||remnants.has(r.key))fail('REMNANT_INVALID','餘料 key、原材引用或可用長度不合法。');
+   remnants.add(r.key);
+  }
+  return {stockKeys:[...stocks.keys()],remnantKeys:[...remnants]};
+ }
+ function validateStockTransition(old,next){
+  for(const s of old.stockTypes){const n=next.stockTypes.find(n=>n.key===s.key);if(n&&old.remnants.some(r=>r.stockType===s.key)&&stockIdentity(s)!==stockIdentity(n))fail('STOCK_TYPE_IN_USE','被宣告餘料引用的截面與材料鍵不可修改。');}
+  return true;
+ }
+ function evaluateRows(rows,settings,p){
+  if(!validSettingsFields(settings))fail('SETTINGS_INVALID','裁切設定欄位或整數不合法。');
+  validateDeclarations(settings.stockTypes,settings.remnants);
+  const rank=new Map([['INCOMPLETE',1],['UNSUPPORTED',2],['INVALID',3]]);
+  const diagnose=(status,code,detail)=>{p.diagnostics.push({status,code,detail});if((rank.get(status)||0)>(rank.get(p.status)||0))p.status=status;};
+  const demands=new Map(),overlength=[];
+  for(const row of Array.from(rows).sort((a,b)=>a.id-b.id)){
+   const terminals=[];let bad=false;
+   const fields=['A','B'];for(const field of fields){const source=row.sources.find(s=>s.target?.field===field);
+    if(!source){terminals.push({kind:'literal'});continue;}
+    if(!Object.hasOwn(source,'terminal')){diagnose('UNSUPPORTED','NATIVE_CAPABILITY',{timberId:row.id,field});bad=true;continue;}
+    if(source.terminal===null){diagnose('INCOMPLETE','MATERIAL_UNRESOLVED',{timberId:row.id,field});bad=true;continue;}
+    terminals.push(source.terminal);
+   }
+   if(bad)continue;
+   let material=null;const [a,b]=terminals;
+   if(a.kind==='material'||b.kind==='material'){
+    if(a.kind!=='material'||b.kind!=='material'||!sameMaterial(a,b)){diagnose('INCOMPLETE','MATERIAL_MIXED',{timberId:row.id});continue;}
+    material={id:a.id,version:a.version,fingerprint:a.fingerprint};
+   }
+   const section=[row.A,row.B],stock=settings.stockTypes.find(s=>sectionKey(s.section)===sectionKey(section)&&sameMaterial(s.material,material));
+   if(!stock){const detail={timberId:row.id,section,material};
+    if(material&&settings.stockTypes.some(s=>s.material?.id===material.id&&s.material.version===material.version&&s.material.fingerprint!==material.fingerprint))diagnose('INCOMPLETE','MATERIAL_FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH',detail);
+    else if(material&&settings.stockTypes.some(s=>sameMaterial(s.material,material)))diagnose('INCOMPLETE','STOCK_SECTION_MISMATCH',detail);
+    else diagnose('INCOMPLETE','NO_STOCK',detail);continue;
+   }
+   const C=row.L+(settings.allowance??0),maximum=Math.max(stock.length-2*stock.endTrim,...settings.remnants.filter(r=>r.stockType===stock.key).map(r=>r.length-r.headTrim-r.tailTrim));
+   if(C>maximum)overlength.push({timberId:row.id,C,maximum});
+   if(!demands.has(stock.key))demands.set(stock.key,[]);demands.get(stock.key).push({timberId:row.id,surface:row.surface,L:row.L,allowance:settings.allowance,C});
+  }
+  for(const detail of overlength)diagnose('INVALID','OVER_LENGTH',detail);
+  const missing=[['kerf',settings.kerf],['allowance',settings.allowance],['minRemnant',settings.minRemnant]].filter(([,value])=>value===null).map(([key])=>key);if(missing.length)diagnose('INCOMPLETE','MISSING_SETTING',missing);
+  if(p.diagnostics.length)return p;
+  for(const stockType of Array.from(settings.stockTypes).sort((a,b)=>a.key-b.key)){
+   const pieces=demands.get(stockType.key);if(!pieces)continue;
+   const candidates=settings.remnants.filter(r=>r.stockType===stockType.key).sort((a,b)=>(a.length-a.headTrim-a.tailTrim)-(b.length-b.headTrim-b.tailTrim)||a.key-b.key)
+    .map(r=>({label:'R'+r.key,key:r.key,length:r.length,headTrim:r.headTrim,tailTrim:r.tailTrim,pieces:[]}));
+   const group={stockTypeKey:stockType.key,stockType:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(stockType)),kerf:settings.kerf,newStockCount:0,stocks:[],unusedRemnants:[],kerfLoss:0,trimLoss:0};
+   let failed=false;
+   for(const demand of Array.from(pieces).sort((a,b)=>b.C-a.C||a.timberId-b.timberId)){
+    const start=s=>s.pieces.length?s.pieces.at(-1).end+settings.kerf:s.headTrim;
+    let s=candidates.find(s=>start(s)+demand.C<=s.length-s.tailTrim);
+    if(!s){if(demand.C>stockType.length-2*stockType.endTrim){diagnose('INCOMPLETE','REMNANT_EXHAUSTED',{stockTypeKey:stockType.key,timberId:demand.timberId,reason:'本次找到的方案無法排入。'});failed=true;break;}
+     s={label:`S${stockType.key}-N${++group.newStockCount}`,length:stockType.length,headTrim:stockType.endTrim,tailTrim:stockType.endTrim,pieces:[]};candidates.push(s);
+    }
+    const x=start(s);s.pieces.push({...demand,seq:s.pieces.length+1,start:x,end:x+demand.C});
+   }
+   if(failed)continue;
+   for(const s of candidates){if(!s.pieces.length){group.unusedRemnants.push(s.key);continue;}delete s.key;
+    const y=s.pieces.at(-1).end,trim=y===s.length-s.tailTrim;
+    s.tail=trim?{kind:s.tailTrim?'trim':'none',consumed:s.tailTrim,cuts:s.tailTrim?1:0}:{kind:'separate',consumed:Math.min(settings.kerf,s.length-y),cuts:1};
+    s.cuts=(s.headTrim>0?1:0)+s.pieces.length-1+s.tail.cuts;
+    const length=s.length-y-s.tail.consumed,usable=Math.max(0,length-s.tailTrim);s.leftover=length?{length,usable,class:usable>=settings.minRemnant?'reusable':'waste'}:null;
+    group.kerfLoss+=(s.pieces.length-1)*settings.kerf+(trim?0:s.tail.consumed);group.trimLoss+=s.headTrim+(trim?s.tail.consumed:0);group.stocks.push(s);

+   for(const s of g.stocks){let y=s.headTrim;const allocations=[],consumptions=[{start:0,end:s.headTrim}];

+     const key={type:'timber',id:p.timberId};checked(()=>identities.add(key));observed.push(key);
+     if(![p.C,p.start,p.end].every(Number.isSafeInteger)||p.C!==p.L+p.allowance||p.seq!==i+1||p.start!==y+(i?g.kerf:0))throw Error('CONSERVATION：件的位置或加工長度不守恆。');
+     if(i)consumptions.push({start:y,end:p.start});

+     allocations.push(allocation);y=p.end;
+    }
+    const trim=y===s.length-s.tailTrim,consumed=trim?s.tailTrim:Math.min(g.kerf,s.length-y),left=s.length-y-consumed;
+    if(s.tail.consumed!==consumed||s.tail.kind!==(trim?s.tailTrim?'trim':'none':'separate')||s.tail.cuts!==(trim?s.tailTrim?1:0:1)||s.cuts!==(s.headTrim?1:0)+s.pieces.length-1+s.tail.cuts||left<0||left!==(s.leftover?.length??0)||s.leftover&&s.leftover.usable!==Math.max(0,left-s.tailTrim))throw Error('CONSERVATION：修整、鋸縫或剩料不守恆。');
+    consumptions.push({start:y,end:y+consumed});
+    containers.push({length:s.length,usable:[s.headTrim,s.length-s.tailTrim],allocations,consumptions,remainder:{start:y+consumed,end:s.length}});
+    kerfLoss+=(s.pieces.length-1)*g.kerf+(trim?0:consumed);trimLoss+=s.headTrim+(trim?consumed:0);
+   }if(g.kerfLoss!==kerfLoss||g.trimLoss!==trimLoss)throw Error('CONSERVATION：耗損彙總不守恆。');
+  }
+  checked(()=>Core.verify({expected:observed,containers}));
+  if(expectedIds&&(identities.size!==expectedIds.length||expectedIds.some(id=>!checked(()=>identities.has({type:'timber',id})))))throw Error('DEMAND_IDENTITY：需求件遺失。');

+ const api={id:'woodworking/1',materialKey,sectionKey,stockIdentity,validSettingsFields,validateDeclarations,validateStockTransition,evaluateRows,verify};

+});

+/* Fixed inputs for the independent cd4bc880 cutting oracle; no product imports. */
+'use strict';
+const material={id:'wood',version:1,fingerprint:'b'.repeat(64)};
+const timber=(id,L,surface=1,A=27,B=33)=>({id,role:'primary',geometry:{surface,A,B,length:L,dir:'+v',side:'+n',origin:[0,0,0],axes:[[0,1,0],[1,0,0],[0,0,1]]}});
+const refs=lengths=>({ok:true,sourceFingerprint:'a'.repeat(64),timbers:lengths.map((L,i)=>timber(i+1,L)),engineeringSources:[]});
+const settings=(length=3636,endTrim=5)=>({kerf:3,allowance:0,minRemnant:300,stockTypes:[{key:1,name:'試用料',section:[27,33],material:null,length,endTrim}],remnants:[],nextStockKey:2,nextRemnantKey:1});
+const request=(scopes=[{surface:1,ids:null}])=>({world:{kind:'live'},scopes});
+function source(r,id,terminal,field='A'){
+ const row={target:{kind:'timber',id,field},path:'不可解析的顯示文字'};
+ if(terminal!==undefined)row.terminal=terminal;
+ r.engineeringSources.push(row);
+}
+function cases(){
+ const out=[];
+ const add=(id,r=refs([100]),s=settings(),q=request())=>{const c={id,action:'evaluate',references:r,settings:s,request:q};out.push(c);return c;};
+ add('ffd-success',refs([2560,2560,890,890,890,876]));
+ add('empty-request',null,settings(),request([]));
+ add('world-kind',refs([100]),settings(),{world:{kind:'model:1'},scopes:[{surface:1,ids:null}]});
+ add('world-unreadable',{...refs([100]),ok:false});
+ add('empty-scope',refs([]));
+ add('unknown-scope',refs([100]),settings(),request([{surface:9,ids:null}]));
+ add('stale-selection',refs([100]),settings(),request([{surface:1,ids:[9]}]));
+ add('surface-mismatch',refs([100]),settings(),request([{surface:2,ids:[1]}]));
+ add('invalid-scope-id',refs([100]),settings(),request([{surface:-1,ids:null}]));
+ add('missing-scopes',refs([100]),settings(),{world:{kind:'live'}});
+ add('scope-capacity',refs([1]),settings(),request(Array.from({length:65},()=>({surface:1,ids:null}))));
+ add('demand-capacity',refs(Array(4097).fill(1)));
+ for(const field of ['kerf','allowance','minRemnant']){const s=settings();s[field]=null;add('missing-'+field,refs([100]),s);}
+ add('over-length',refs([3700]));
+ {const r=refs([3700,100]),s=settings();r.timbers[1].geometry.A=30;r.timbers[1].geometry.B=36;s.kerf=null;add('diagnostic-phase-order',r,s);}
+ for(const [name,terminal]of [['unresolved-terminal',null],['mixed-material',{kind:'material',...material}],['missing-terminal',undefined]]){const r=refs([100]);source(r,1,terminal);add(name,r);}
+ {const r=refs([100,100]),s=settings();for(const field of ['A','B'])source(r,1,{kind:'material',...material},field);r.timbers[0].geometry.A=33;r.timbers[0].geometry.B=27;s.stockTypes.push({...s.stockTypes[0],key:2,material});s.nextStockKey=3;add('material-triplet-and-unordered-section',r,s);}
+ for(const [name,mat,section]of [['no-stock',null,[27,33]],['material-fingerprint',{...material,fingerprint:'c'.repeat(64)},[27,33]],['stock-section',material,[30,36]]]){const r=refs([100]),s=settings();for(const field of ['A','B'])source(r,1,{kind:'material',...material},field);s.stockTypes[0].material=mat;s.stockTypes[0].section=section;add(name,r,s);}
+ {const r=refs([3200,3100,3200,3100]),s=settings(3000,5);for(const id of [3,4])for(const field of ['A','B'])source(r,id,{kind:'material',...material},field);s.stockTypes.push({...s.stockTypes[0],key:2,material});s.nextStockKey=3;s.remnants=[{key:1,stockType:1,length:3300,headTrim:0,tailTrim:5},{key:2,stockType:2,length:3300,headTrim:0,tailTrim:5}];s.nextRemnantKey=3;add('remnant-exhaustion-every-group',r,s);}
+ {const r=refs([100]);r.timbers=[timber(0,100,0),timber(1,100,0),timber(7,100,2)];add('zero-and-union-identity',r,settings(),request([{surface:0,ids:null},{surface:0,ids:[0]},{surface:2,ids:[7]}]));}
+ add('exact-finish-trim',refs([3626]));
+ for(const [name,last]of [['original-end',1497],['short-last-kerf',1496],['normal-last-kerf',1490]])add(name,refs([1500,last]),settings(3000,0));

+ {const s=settings();s.stockTypes[0].name=' =HYPERLINK("x")\n木材';add('csv-formula-and-newline',refs([3626]),s);}
+ {const r=refs([2560,2560,890,890,890,876]),s=settings();r.timbers.reverse();s.stockTypes.push({...s.stockTypes[0],key:2,section:[28,33]});s.nextStockKey=3;s.stockTypes.reverse();s.remnants=[{key:2,stockType:2,length:700,headTrim:0,tailTrim:0},{key:1,stockType:1,length:1000,headTrim:0,tailTrim:5}];s.nextRemnantKey=3;add('reversed-declarations-and-selection',r,s,request([{surface:1,ids:[6,4,2]},{surface:1,ids:[5,3,1]}]));}
+ {const r=refs([100]);r.timbers[0].geometry.A=0;add('typed-row-not-ready',r);}
+ for(const terminal of [true,false]){const r=refs([100]),s=settings();s.stockTypes[0].material=material;r.assembly={instances:[{value:{source:{id:4,bindingId:9,rule:{id:7,version:2,fingerprint:'b'.repeat(64)}}},members:[{memberId:12,timberId:1}]}],rules:[{value:{id:7,version:2,members:[{id:12,slotId:5}]},fingerprint:'b'.repeat(64)}],bindings:[{value:{id:9,slots:[{slotId:5,A:{kind:'material',id:material.id,version:1},B:{kind:'material',id:material.id,version:1}}]},complete:true,fingerprint:'c'.repeat(64),effective:[{slotId:5,A:{value:27,path:'不可解析的來源'},B:{value:33,path:'不可解析的來源'}}]}]};if(terminal)for(const field of ['A','B'])r.assembly.bindings[0].effective[0][field].terminal={kind:'material',...material};add(terminal?'owned-material-terminal':'owned-missing-terminal',r,s);}
+ for(const [name,edit]of [
+  ['kerf-zero',s=>s.kerf=0],['negative-allowance',s=>s.allowance=-1],['minimum-zero',s=>s.minRemnant=0],

+  ['trim-no-usable',s=>s.stockTypes[0].endTrim=1818],['unknown-setting',s=>s.extra=true]]){const s=settings();edit(s);add('reject-'+name,refs([100]),s);}

+function verifyCases(){return [
+ {id:'duplicate-before-same-piece-invalid-geometry',planCase:'ffd-success',changes:[{path:['groups',0,'stocks',0,'pieces',1,'timberId'],value:1},{path:['groups',0,'stocks',0,'pieces',1,'end'],value:3459}]},

+ {id:'later-group-duplicate-before-same-piece-geometry',planCase:'material-triplet-and-unordered-section',changes:[{path:['groups',1,'stocks',0,'pieces',0,'timberId'],value:2},{path:['groups',1,'stocks',0,'pieces',0,'end'],value:106}]},
+];}
+function applyChanges(plan,changes){for(const change of changes){const owner=change.path.slice(0,-1).reduce((v,k)=>v[k],plan);owner[change.path.at(-1)]=change.value;}return plan;}
+module.exports={cases,verifyCases,applyChanges,material,refs,settings,request,source,timber};

diff --git a/tests/generate_cutting_legacy_fixture.js b/tests/generate_cutting_legacy_fixture.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..f37479076280dddd60bb214f62ad7eaf523abfcf
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/generate_cutting_legacy_fixture.js
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+/* Regenerate only from the fixed pre-layering Git blobs, never the working tree. */
+'use strict';
+const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),vm=require('node:vm'),cp=require('node:child_process'),crypto=require('node:crypto');
+const {cases,verifyCases,applyChanges}=require('./cutting_legacy_cases.js');
+const PIN='cd4bc8803016943e6ec7bf98f0c90bc0312cfa06';
+const ROOT=path.resolve(__dirname,'..'),OUTPUT=path.join(__dirname,'fixtures/cutting_legacy_cd4bc880.json');
+const SOURCES=[
+ {path:'geometry-web/cutting-settings.js',blob:'d7cac099298d42018c5a278a5217bc625d8598a7',sha256:'23f44ae9717405bbc5bf95ca32f0cda47af0e25104972d0d984ed5b85d94e265'},
+ {path:'geometry-web/timber-schedule.js',blob:'c634957ad21e16377fab55fcf7edc7c5e6239392',sha256:'bb1558ff482591b2c4085a1f20c46e50999db7bfa2be748d74f131e85726bd9c'},
+ {path:'geometry-web/cutting-plan.js',blob:'feb3b9ab98e73f9cbe7e022f71e3d7e354b71c73',sha256:'d160c37d1c4b4b283082aa86d86df7c431f806f4104d2c6f3da96f42c6a51bf5'},
+];
+const sha=b=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
+async function generate(){
+ const context=vm.createContext({crypto:crypto.webcrypto,TextEncoder});
+ for(const source of SOURCES){
+  const blob=cp.execFileSync('git',['rev-parse',PIN+':'+source.path],{cwd:ROOT,encoding:'utf8'}).trim();
+  const bytes=cp.execFileSync('git',['show',PIN+':'+source.path],{cwd:ROOT});
+  if(blob!==source.blob||sha(bytes)!==source.sha256)throw Error('Pinned legacy source mismatch: '+source.path);
+  vm.runInContext(bytes.toString('utf8'),context,{filename:PIN+'/'+source.path});
+ }
+ const entries=[];
+ for(const input of cases()){
+  const before=JSON.stringify(input);context.input=JSON.parse(before);let output;
+  try{
+   if(input.action==='transition')output={value:JSON.stringify(vm.runInContext('CuttingSettings.transition(input.previous,input.value)',context))};
+   else{
+    context.plan=await vm.runInContext('CuttingPlan.evaluate(input.references,input.settings,input.request)',context);
+    output={plan:JSON.stringify(context.plan),csv:context.plan.status==='COMPLETE'?vm.runInContext('CuttingPlan.csv(plan)',context):null};
+   }
+  }catch(error){output={error:{code:error.code??null,message:error.message}};}
+  if(JSON.stringify(context.input)!==before)throw Error('Legacy evaluation mutated '+input.id);
+  let nativeRows;
+  const plan=output.plan?JSON.parse(output.plan):null;
+  if(plan&&plan.status!=='EMPTY'&&!plan.diagnostics.some(d=>/^(CAPACITY|WORLD_|SCOPE_|ROW_NOT_READY)/.test(d.code))){
+   try{
+    nativeRows=vm.runInContext('(()=>{const rows=new Map();for(const scope of input.request.scopes)for(const row of TimberSchedule.derive(input.references,scope.surface,scope.ids).rows)rows.set(row.id,row);return [...rows.values()];})()',context);
+   }catch(_){ /* No independent valid row input for this host rejection. */ }
+  }
+  entries.push({id:input.id,inputSha256:sha(Buffer.from(before)),...output,...(nativeRows?{nativeRows}:{} )});
+ }
+ const verification=[];
+ for(const input of verifyCases()){
+  context.plan=applyChanges(JSON.parse(entries.find(c=>c.id===input.planCase).plan),input.changes);
+  const output={id:input.id,inputSha256:sha(JSON.stringify(input))};
+  for(const method of ['verify','csv'])try{output[method]={value:JSON.stringify(vm.runInContext('CuttingPlan.'+method+'(plan)',context))};}catch(error){output[method]={error:{code:error.code??null,message:error.message}};}
+  verification.push(output);
+ }
+ return {schema:'cutting-legacy-oracle/1',sourceCommit:PIN,sources:SOURCES,inputHelperSha256:sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname,'cutting_legacy_cases.js'))),cases:entries,verification};
+}
+if(require.main===module)generate().then(data=>{
+ const bytes=Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(data,null,2)+'\n');
+ if(process.argv.slice(2).join(' ')==='--check'){
+  if(!fs.existsSync(OUTPUT)||!bytes.equals(fs.readFileSync(OUTPUT)))throw Error('Independent legacy fixture differs');
+ }else if(process.argv.length===2){fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(OUTPUT),{recursive:true});fs.writeFileSync(OUTPUT,bytes);}
+ else throw Error('Usage: node tests/generate_cutting_legacy_fixture.js [--check]');
+ console.log(JSON.stringify({sourceCommit:PIN,cases:data.cases.length,sha256:sha(bytes),mode:process.argv.includes('--check')?'verified':'generated'}));
+}).catch(e=>{console.error(e);process.exitCode=1;});
+module.exports={PIN,SOURCES,generate};

完整修法、實跑命令／結果及 T-only 紅案：[交付說明](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/357#issuecomment-6503387853)。本補丁未含反斜線 u 控制跳脫。保存時取下方 diff 原文，保留最後 LF。

```diff
diff --git a/tests/test_cutting_layering.js b/tests/test_cutting_layering.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2e31c59e9f26e9d321def6d2791219ecef8c68e5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_cutting_layering.js
@@ -0,0 +1,194 @@
+'use strict';
+const {test}=require('node:test'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
+const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),vm=require('node:vm'),crypto=require('node:crypto'),os=require('node:os'),cp=require('node:child_process');
+const ROOT=path.resolve(process.env.GEOMETRY_CUTTING_TEST_ROOT||path.join(__dirname,'..'));
+const {cases,verifyCases,applyChanges}=require('./cutting_legacy_cases.js');
+const golden=require('./fixtures/cutting_legacy_cd4bc880.json');
+const clone=v=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v)),sha=b=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
+const read=p=>{assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(ROOT,p)),'required layer source: '+p);return fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT,p),'utf8');};
+const K_PATH='geometry-core/interval-allocation.js',W_PATH='geometry-templates/woodworking/cutting.js';
+function load(relative){assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(ROOT,relative)),'required layer source: '+relative);return require(path.join(ROOT,relative));}
+function standalone(){
+ const context=vm.createContext({TextEncoder});
+ vm.runInContext(read(K_PATH),context,{filename:K_PATH});
+ vm.runInContext(read(W_PATH),context,{filename:W_PATH});
+ return context;
+}
+function hostContext({kernel=true,template=true,application=true}={}){
+ const context=vm.createContext({crypto:crypto.webcrypto,TextEncoder});
+ if(kernel)vm.runInContext(read(K_PATH),context);
+ vm.runInContext(read('geometry-templates/woodworking/frontend.js'),context);
+ if(template)vm.runInContext(read(W_PATH),context);
+ if(application)vm.runInContext(read('geometry-app/application.js'),context);
+ vm.runInContext(read('geometry-web/timber-schedule.js'),context);
+ vm.runInContext(read('geometry-web/cutting-settings.js'),context);
+ vm.runInContext(read('geometry-web/cutting-plan.js'),context);
+ return context;
+}
+
+test('R357 legacy oracle is pinned independently with all fixed input hashes',()=>{
+ assert.equal(golden.sourceCommit,'cd4bc8803016943e6ec7bf98f0c90bc0312cfa06');
+ assert.deepEqual(golden.sources.map(s=>s.blob),['d7cac099298d42018c5a278a5217bc625d8598a7','c634957ad21e16377fab55fcf7edc7c5e6239392','feb3b9ab98e73f9cbe7e022f71e3d7e354b71c73']);
+ assert.equal(golden.inputHelperSha256,sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname,'cutting_legacy_cases.js'))));
+ assert.equal(golden.cases.length,53);
+ assert.deepEqual(cases().map(c=>[c.id,sha(JSON.stringify(c))]),golden.cases.map(c=>[c.id,c.inputSha256]));
+});
+test('R357 all 53 legacy JSON outputs, CSV bytes and rejection order remain exact',async()=>{
+ const C=load('geometry-web/cutting-plan.js'),S=load('geometry-web/cutting-settings.js');
+ for(const input of cases()){
+  const expected=golden.cases.find(c=>c.id===input.id),before=JSON.stringify(input);let actual;
+  try{
+   if(input.action==='transition')actual={value:JSON.stringify(S.transition(input.previous,input.value))};
+   else{const plan=await C.evaluate(input.references,input.settings,input.request);actual={plan:JSON.stringify(plan),csv:plan.status==='COMPLETE'?C.csv(plan):null};}
+  }catch(error){actual={error:{code:error.code??null,message:error.message}};}
+  const wanted=expected.error?{error:expected.error}:expected.value?{value:expected.value}:{plan:expected.plan,csv:expected.csv};
+  assert.deepEqual(actual,wanted,input.id);
+  if(actual.csv!==null&&actual.csv!==undefined)assert.ok(Buffer.from(actual.csv).equals(Buffer.from(expected.csv)),input.id+' UTF-8 CSV bytes');
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(input),before,input.id+' is read-only');
+ }
+});
+test('R357 W evaluates native rows without H, DOM, Window, Schedule or Settings',()=>{
+ const context=standalone(),W=context.GeometryWoodworkingCutting;
+ assert.equal(W.id,'woodworking/1');assert.ok(Object.isFrozen(W));
+ for(const name of ['window','document','Window','GeometryApplication','CuttingSettings','TimberSchedule','CuttingPlan','require'])assert.equal(typeof context[name],'undefined',name+' absent');
+ let count=0;
+ for(const expected of golden.cases.filter(c=>c.nativeRows)){
+  const input=cases().find(c=>c.id===expected.id),old=JSON.parse(expected.plan);
+  const rows=clone(expected.nativeRows),settings=clone(input.settings),before=JSON.stringify([rows,settings]);
+  const envelope={status:'EMPTY',diagnostics:[],worldFingerprint:input.references.sourceFingerprint,settingsFingerprint:old.settingsFingerprint,label:'本次找到的方案',groups:[]};
+  assert.equal(W.evaluateRows(rows,settings,envelope),envelope,'explicit caller-owned result');
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(envelope),expected.plan,expected.id+' standalone W numeric/status/ordering parity');
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify([rows,settings]),before,expected.id+' standalone W inputs are read-only');count++;
+ }
+ assert.equal(count,29,'all independently derived valid-row cases must run');
+});
+test('R357 W delegates identity, each allocation and full conservation to shared K',()=>{
+ const context=vm.createContext({TextEncoder});
+ vm.runInContext(read(K_PATH),context);
+ const K=context.GeometryIntervalAllocation,calls={identity:0,allocation:0,whole:0};
+ const observed={...K,createIdentityTracker(){calls.identity++;return K.createIdentityTracker();},verifyAllocation(...args){calls.allocation++;return K.verifyAllocation(...args);},verify(...args){calls.whole++;return K.verify(...args);}};
+ Object.values(observed).filter(v=>typeof v==='function').forEach(Object.freeze);Object.freeze(observed);
+ // Instrument the dependency edge while actual K and W execute in one realm.
+ // This fixture uses W's CommonJS composition, not a second implementation.
+ context.module={exports:{}};
+ context.require=relative=>{assert.equal(relative,'../../geometry-core/interval-allocation.js');return observed;};
+ vm.runInContext(read(W_PATH),context);
+ const plan=JSON.parse(golden.cases.find(c=>c.id==='ffd-success').plan);
+ assert.equal(context.module.exports.verify(plan),true);
+ assert.deepEqual(calls,{identity:1,allocation:6,whole:1},'W must use the shared K validators on the actual plan');
+});
+test('R357 public verify and CSV preserve pinned legacy error priority across pieces and groups',()=>{
+ const C=load('geometry-web/cutting-plan.js');
+ assert.equal(golden.verification.length,4);
+ for(const input of verifyCases()){
+  const expected=golden.verification.find(c=>c.id===input.id);
+  assert.equal(sha(JSON.stringify(input)),expected.inputSha256,input.id+' fixed mutation input');
+  const plan=applyChanges(JSON.parse(golden.cases.find(c=>c.id===input.planCase).plan),input.changes);
+  for(const method of ['verify','csv']){
+   let actual;try{actual={value:JSON.stringify(C[method](plan))};}catch(error){actual={error:{code:error.code??null,message:error.message}};}
+   assert.deepEqual(actual,expected[method],input.id+' '+method+' error order and text');
+  }
+ }
+});
+test('R357 public H uses the same frozen W and cannot replace policy through request fields',async()=>{
+ const C=load('geometry-web/cutting-plan.js'),W=load(W_PATH),application=load('geometry-app/application.js');
+ assert.equal(application.cutting,W);assert.ok(Object.isFrozen(application));
+ assert.throws(()=>{application.cutting={evaluateRows(){return {status:'COMPLETE'};}};},TypeError);
+ assert.throws(()=>{W.evaluateRows=()=>({status:'COMPLETE'});},TypeError);
+ const input=cases().find(c=>c.id==='mixed-material');
+ const request={...input.request,template:'other/1',cutting:{evaluateRows(){assert.fail('request cannot override W');}},evaluateRows(){assert.fail('request cannot override W');}};
+ assert.equal(JSON.stringify(await C.evaluate(input.references,input.settings,request)),golden.cases.find(c=>c.id===input.id).plan);
+ const browser=hostContext();assert.equal(browser.GeometryApplication.cutting,browser.GeometryWoodworkingCutting);
+ assert.throws(()=>vm.runInContext('"use strict"; GeometryApplication.cutting = {};',browser),/read only|readonly|Cannot assign/i);
+ assert.throws(()=>vm.runInContext('"use strict"; GeometryWoodworkingCutting = {};',browser),/read only|readonly|Cannot assign/i);
+});
+test('R357 public composition refuses missing W and W refuses missing K',()=>{
+ assert.throws(()=>hostContext({template:false}),/缺少固定裁切模板/);
+ assert.throws(()=>hostContext({kernel:false}),/缺少共用區間驗證器/);
+});
+
+const key=(id=0,extra={})=>({type:'neutral-demand',id,...extra});
+function allocation(){return {expected:[key(0),key(1)],containers:[{length:40,usable:[2,37],allocations:[{key:key(0),length:10,start:2,end:12},{key:key(1),length:10,start:15,end:25}],consumptions:[{start:0,end:2},{start:12,end:15},{start:25,end:27}],remainder:{start:27,end:40}}]};}
+test('R357 K neutral allocation conserves explicit intervals without woodworking defaults',()=>{
+ const K=load(K_PATH),input=allocation(),saved=JSON.stringify(input);assert.equal(K.verify(input),true);assert.equal(JSON.stringify(input),saved);
+ const maximum=Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,large={expected:[key('external')],containers:[{length:maximum,usable:[0,maximum],allocations:[{key:key('external'),length:maximum-1,start:0,end:maximum-1}],consumptions:[],remainder:{start:maximum-1,end:maximum}}]};
+ assert.equal(K.verify(large),true,'K does not inherit the W stock dimension limit');
+});
+test('R357 K typed identities retain type, id type, owner, version and nested source identity',()=>{
+ const K=load(K_PATH),keys=[key(0),{type:'other-demand',id:0},key('0'),key(0,{owner:{type:'container',id:8},version:1}),key(0,{owner:{type:'container',id:9},version:1}),key(0,{owner:{type:'container',id:8},version:2})];
+ const input={expected:keys,containers:[{length:6,usable:[0,6],allocations:keys.map((k,i)=>({key:clone(k),length:1,start:i,end:i+1})),consumptions:[],remainder:{start:6,end:6}}]};
+ assert.equal(K.verify(input),true);
+ input.containers[0].allocations[3].key={version:1,owner:{id:8,type:'container'},id:0,type:'neutral-demand'};
+ assert.equal(K.verify(input),true,'property order does not create another identity');
+ input.containers[0].allocations[3].key.owner.id=9;
+ assert.throws(()=>K.verify(input),/DEMAND_IDENTITY/,'same bare id with changed owner cannot be substituted');
+});
+test('R357 K rejects duplicate, absent, unexpected and untyped demand identities',()=>{
+ const K=load(K_PATH);
+ const edits=[p=>p.expected.push(key(0)),p=>p.expected.push(key(2)),p=>p.expected.pop(),p=>p.containers[0].allocations[1].key=key(0),p=>p.containers[0].allocations[1].key=1,p=>p.containers[0].allocations[1].key={id:1},p=>p.containers[0].allocations[1].key={type:'neutral-demand',id:-1},p=>p.containers.push(clone(p.containers[0]))];
+ for(const [i,edit]of edits.entries()){const p=allocation();edit(p);assert.throws(()=>K.verify(p),/DEMAND_IDENTITY/,'identity negative '+i);}
+});
+test('R357 K typed identity rejects inherited, hidden and accessor identity fields',()=>{
+ const K=load(K_PATH);
+ const nested=[1,2];Object.defineProperty(nested,'revision',{value:3,enumerable:false});
+ const custom=Object.assign(Object.create(Object.assign(Object.create(null),{sourceVersion:9})),key(0));
+ const keys=[Object.create({type:'neutral-demand',id:0}),Object.defineProperty({id:0},'type',{value:'neutral-demand',enumerable:false}),Object.defineProperty({type:'neutral-demand'},'id',{value:0,enumerable:false}),Object.defineProperty(key(0),'owner',{value:4,enumerable:false}),Object.defineProperty(key(0),'version',{get(){assert.fail('identity getter must never run');},enumerable:true}),{get type(){assert.fail('type getter must never run');},id:0},{type:'neutral-demand',get id(){assert.fail('id getter must never run');}},key(0,{owner:nested}),custom];
+ for(const [i,k]of keys.entries()){
+  const p=allocation();p.expected[0]=k;p.containers[0].allocations[0].key=k;
+  assert.throws(()=>K.verify(p),/DEMAND_IDENTITY/,'opaque key negative '+i);
+ }
+});
+test('R357 K rejects missing consumption, overlap, bounds and noninteger conservation',()=>{
+ const K=load(K_PATH);
+ const edits=[p=>p.containers[0].consumptions.pop(),p=>delete p.containers[0].consumptions,p=>delete p.containers[0].remainder,p=>p.containers[0].consumptions[2]={start:24,end:26},p=>p.containers[0].remainder.end=41,p=>p.containers[0].allocations[0].start=1,p=>p.containers[0].allocations[0].length=9,p=>p.containers[0].allocations[0].length=0,p=>p.containers[0].length=40.5,p=>p.containers[0].allocations[0].end=12.5,p=>p.containers[0].usable=[3,37],p=>p.containers[0].usable=[2,24],p=>p.containers[0].usable=[-1,37],p=>p.containers[0].consumptions[0].start=-1,p=>p.containers[0].length=Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER+1,p=>{const c=p.containers[0];[c.remainder,c.consumptions[0]]=[c.consumptions[0],c.remainder];}];
+ for(const [i,edit]of edits.entries()){const p=allocation();edit(p);assert.throws(()=>K.verify(p),/CONSERVATION/,'conservation negative '+i);}
+});
+test('R357 W verifies fixed processing semantics before H exports any corrupted plan',async()=>{
+ const C=load('geometry-web/cutting-plan.js'),W=load(W_PATH);
+ const original=JSON.parse(golden.cases.find(c=>c.id==='ffd-success').plan);
+ const edits=[p=>p.groups[0].stocks[0].pieces[0].end++,p=>p.groups[0].stocks[0].tail.consumed++,p=>p.groups[0].stocks[0].cuts++,p=>p.groups[0].kerfLoss++,p=>p.groups[0].trimLoss++,p=>p.groups[0].stocks[0].pieces[1].timberId=1];
+ for(const [i,edit]of edits.entries()){const p=clone(original);edit(p);assert.throws(()=>W.verify(p),/CONSERVATION|DEMAND_IDENTITY/,'W negative '+i);assert.throws(()=>C.csv(p),/CONSERVATION|DEMAND_IDENTITY/,'H CSV negative '+i);}
+ assert.throws(()=>W.verify(original,[1,2,3,4,5,6,9]),/DEMAND_IDENTITY/,'expected demand cannot disappear');
+});
+test('R357 four real layer mutations must fail named behavior assertions',()=>{
+ const mutations=[
+  {id:'K-no-op',file:K_PATH,before:'function verify(value) {',after:'function verify(value) { return true;',pattern:'R357 K rejects missing consumption'},
+  {id:'K-flatten-identity',file:K_PATH,before:'return canonical(key);',after:'return JSON.stringify([key.type,key.id]);',pattern:'R357 K typed identities retain'},
+  {id:'W-bypass-K',file:W_PATH,before:'checked(()=>Core.verify({expected:observed,containers}));',after:'void 0;',pattern:'R357 W delegates identity'},
+  {id:'H-bypass-W',file:'geometry-web/cutting-plan.js',before:'return Policy.evaluateRows([...rows.values()],settings,p);',after:'return p;',pattern:'R357 all 53 legacy JSON outputs'},
+ ];
+ const work=fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'geometry-cutting-layer-mutations-'));
+ const files=[K_PATH,W_PATH,'geometry-app/application.js','geometry-templates/woodworking/frontend.js','geometry-web/cutting-settings.js','geometry-web/cutting-plan.js','geometry-web/timber-schedule.js'];
+ const tests=['test_cutting_layering.js','cutting_legacy_cases.js','fixtures/cutting_legacy_cd4bc880.json'];
+ const evidence=process.env.GEOMETRY_CUTTING_MUTATION_EVIDENCE;
+ const report={schema:'cutting-layer-mutations/1',sources:{},tests:{},results:[]};
+ const save=(name,body)=>{if(evidence){fs.mkdirSync(evidence,{recursive:true});fs.writeFileSync(path.join(evidence,name),body);}};
+ try{
+  for(const file of files){const text=read(file);fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(path.join(work,file)),{recursive:true});fs.writeFileSync(path.join(work,file),text);report.sources[file]=sha(text);}
+  for(const file of tests){const target=path.join(work,'tests',file),bytes=fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname,file));fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(target),{recursive:true});fs.writeFileSync(target,bytes);report.tests[file]=sha(bytes);}
+  function run(pattern){
+   const env={...process.env,GEOMETRY_CUTTING_TEST_ROOT:work,GEOMETRY_CUTTING_MUTATION_EVIDENCE:''};
+   // A fresh CLI judge must not inherit node:test's internal child marker;
+   // otherwise Node skips the entire child suite with a successful exit.
+   delete env.NODE_TEST_CONTEXT;
+   return cp.spawnSync(process.execPath,['--test','--test-reporter=tap','--test-name-pattern',pattern,path.join(work,'tests/test_cutting_layering.js')],{cwd:work,encoding:'utf8',timeout:60000,env});
+  }
+  const baseline=run(mutations.map(m=>m.pattern).join('|'));save('baseline.log',baseline.stdout+baseline.stderr);
+  assert.equal(baseline.status,0,'real unmutated baseline must complete: '+baseline.stdout+baseline.stderr);
+  for(const m of mutations)assert.ok([...baseline.stdout.matchAll(/^ok \d+ - (.+)$/gm)].some(match=>match[1].startsWith(m.pattern)),m.id+' named baseline must actually execute');
+  report.baseline={exitCode:baseline.status,namedTests:mutations.map(m=>m.pattern)};
+  for(const m of mutations){
+   const file=path.join(work,m.file),original=fs.readFileSync(file,'utf8');
+   assert.equal(original.split(m.before).length-1,1,m.id+' exact mutation anchor');
+   fs.writeFileSync(file,original.replace(m.before,m.after));let result;
+   try{result=run(m.pattern);}finally{fs.writeFileSync(file,original);}
+   const log=(result.stdout||'')+(result.stderr||''),named=[...log.matchAll(/^not ok \d+ - (.+)$/gm)].some(match=>match[1].startsWith(m.pattern));
+   const blocked=/SyntaxError|MODULE_NOT_FOUND|ReferenceError|TypeError|uncaughtException|unhandledRejection/.test(log);
+   const cause=/AssertionError/.test(log)?'assertion':/CONSERVATION|DEMAND_IDENTITY/.test(log)?'invariant':null;
+   const killed=result.status===1&&named&&!blocked&&cause!==null;
+   save(m.id+'.log',log);report.results.push({id:m.id,source:m.file,sourceSha256:sha(original),mutationSha256:sha(m.before+'\0'+m.after),testPattern:m.pattern,exitCode:result.status,signal:result.signal,detectedBy:cause,status:killed?'killed':'not-killed'});
+   assert.ok(killed,m.id+' must be killed by the named behavior test: '+log);
+  }
+  assert.equal(report.results.length,4);save('results.json',JSON.stringify(report,null,2)+'\n');
+ }finally{fs.rmSync(work,{recursive:true,force:true});}
+});

+  vm.runInContext(read('geometry-core/interval-allocation.js'), context);
+  vm.runInContext(read('geometry-templates/woodworking/cutting.js'), context);
   if (loadTemplate) vm.runInContext(read('geometry-templates/woodworking/frontend.js'), context);
   vm.runInContext(read('geometry-app/application.js'), context);
   return context;
```
